Abstract: Multiscale modeling of complex chemical systems requires algorithms that operate coherently across electronic, atomistic, mesoscopic, and continuum scales. While quantum algorithms have been proposed for each regime, no systematic framework exists to compose them across scale boundaries. Here, we identify the conditions under which fault-tolerant quantum algorithms might preserve scale-specific quantum advantages. We map quantum phase estimation, Hamiltonian simulation with Gibbs state preparation, quantum random walks, and quantum partial differential equation solvers onto electronic structure, molecular dynamics, mesoscopic kinetics, and continuum reactor physics, respectively. Crucially, these correspondences do not imply unconditional end-to-end quantum advantage; speedups depend heavily on state preparation, memory architectures, matrix conditioning, and classical readout costs. Six unresolved questions define this composition problem, illustrated via a quantum hierarchy for \ce{CO} oxidation over \ce{Pt(111)}. We propose viewing inter-scale transfer as a quantum channel composition problem at the interface of algorithm design and non-equilibrium statistical mechanics, and ask whether information loss at scale boundaries is intrinsic to multiscale modeling or merely a consequence of lossy classical transduction between algorithmic layers. The resulting roadmap suggests that multiscale quantum advantage is governed primarily by the structure of information transfer between algorithmic layers, rather than by performance at individual scales alone.

</details>

Result: 在洲际距离上，所提方案的代价最优中继器密度比近期[[48,6,8]]基准低约一个数量级，比同等规模对称编码低约一半。

Conclusion: 通过联合设计传输与计算，利用编码不对称性可显著提升分布式量子计算的传输效率，且所提框架支持客户端无需解码器的分布式量子计算。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computation needs to move logical qubits across lossy optical links, yet this transmission layer is usually designed separately from the computation it serves. We treat the two together by recognizing that a measurement-based quantum repeater is a two-dimensional code foliated along the transmission axis, so that the dominant channel loss is concentrated on the transmitted sector while the locally measured qubits are largely spared. Matching a code's distance to this structural asymmetry, we show that a rectangular Bacon-Shor subsystem code transmits a logical qubit markedly more efficiently than transmission-unaware encodings. Over continental distances, its cost-optimal repeater density is about an order of magnitude lower than that of a recent $[[48,6,8]]$ benchmark at comparable transmission rate, and roughly half that of a symmetric code of equal size. Moreover, we extend the framework to a central-to-client round trip in which a code-level, distance-preserving code switch joins the transmission legs to the client's computation, and joint decoding of the heterogeneous syndrome record at the central node lets distributed quantum computation proceed with a decoder-free client.

</details>


### [97] [Deployment of Entanglement-Based QKD in Financial Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11252)
*Mirela Selimović,Roman Solar,Jonathan Gruner,Sebastian Mair,Mario Wenzl,Thomas Heine,Matej Pivoluska,Rupert Ursin,Sebastian Philipp Neumann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在22公里光纤链路上实现了基于纠缠的量子密钥分发（eQKD），在8 dB损耗下持续四个月生成安全密钥，平均速率63.8 kb/s，并成功用于建立VPN隧道，展示了eQKD在金融基础设施中的实际应用可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证纠缠量子密钥分发（eQKD）在真实高安全金融基础设施中的长期稳定运行可行性，推动量子通信从实验室走向实际应用。

Method: 在22 km光纤链路（8 dB损耗）上部署偏振纠缠eQKD系统，采用主动偏振控制、基于纠缠光子对时间关联的同步方案（亚300 ps精度），无需偏振导引激光或外部高精度时间参考，实现全自动运行。

Result: 四个月持续运行，平均密钥率63.8 kb/s，总运行时间93.7%（量子光学组件零故障），QBER低于2%的时间占比97.4%，时间同步精度<300 ps，密钥存入管理系统并用于VPN隧道。

Conclusion: eQKD可实际集成到运营级金融基础设施中，具备长期稳定性和实用性。

Abstract: We demonstrate the feasibility of entanglement-based quantum key distribution (eQKD) in high-security financial infrastructure over a 22 km fiber link with 8 dB loss between two data centers using polarization entanglement. The fully automated system continuously generated secure keys for four months at an average rate of 63.8 kb/s, which were stored into a key management system and consumed to establish a VPN tunnel. The setup achieved 93.7% total up-time, with no downtime caused by the quantum optical components. Active polarization control kept the quantum bit error rate below 2% for 97.4% of the time and timing synchronization based on the entangled photon pairs' intrinsic temporal correlations achieved sub-300 ps precision. Our standalone system requires neither polarized guide lasers nor external high-precision time references. These results show practical integration of eQKD into operational financial infrastructure.

</details>


### [98] [Fixed-Protocol Amortized MPS Tomography with Conformalized Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11273)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出量子态层析在样本稀缺时，先验记忆可能被误认为是测量效率。作者提出基于固定协议、不依赖排列不变编码器的MPS估计器，关键在于使用信息性局域Pauli测量设计，而非随机测量，从而显著提升保真度（高达0.95），并通过dropout集成和保形校准提供可靠的不确定度区间，在IBM硬件上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态层析面临样本匮乏的问题，且实际制备的量子态位于一个狭窄的可学习流形上。现有方法在少测量下取得的高保真度可能并非源于真正的测量效率，而是模型对先验分布的记忆。因此需要一种能真正利用测量信息、而非仅依赖先验的层析方法。

Method: 基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）核心参数化，提出固定协议摊销估计器（Approach B），使用规范不变的保真度损失训练，采用简单MLP而非排列不变集编码器；关键创新在于使用信息性局域Pauli测量集而非随机测量串；通过dropout集成和保形校准生成覆盖区间。

Result: 信息性Pauli测量使保真度达约0.95，比纯先验提升高达0.59；经混洗测量控制检验确证了测量利用效率。dropout集成经保形校准后给出约90%覆盖率的区间。系统扩展至n=10时保真度0.90（增益随n增长），键维χ=4时保真度0.88；参数化复杂度多项式级，可原生收缩至20量子比特；在IBM硬件上对5个态测得保真度0.97。

Conclusion: 测量设计是决定量子态层析效率的关键杠杆，信息性局域测量而非随机测量可显著提升性能；所提方法具备可扩展性、多项式复杂度，并在真实硬件上验证有效；不确定性量化能力使其能对从未测量的可观测量提供可靠区间。

Abstract: Quantum state tomography is sample-starved, and the states one prepares live on a narrow, learnable manifold. A $k{=}0$ prior-only control shows that on concentrated families a prior estimate is already near-optimal, so ``high fidelity at few measurements'' can be family memorization rather than tomography; genuine measurement-efficiency needs a model that conditions on the measurements and demonstrably uses them. On a shared matrix-product-state (MPS) core parameterization we study two routes. Approach~A learns a generative prior over MPS cores with measurement-guided posterior inference (gold-standard-validated, but whose few-measurement accuracy the control shows is largely the prior). Approach~B, our main proposal, is a \emph{fixed-protocol amortized} MPS estimator trained once with a gauge-invariant fidelity loss; we deliberately do not rest it on a permutation-invariant set encoder (a plain MLP matches it). The decisive lever is the measurement design: motivated by the fact that local reduced density matrices determine a $χ$-MPS, conditioning on an \emph{informative local} Pauli set rather than random strings turns a modest, memorization-prone estimator into a high-fidelity one ($\approx\!0.95$, up to $+0.59$ over prior-only, decisively passing a shuffled-measurement control). A dropout ensemble, conformally recalibrated, gives $\approx\!90\%$-coverage intervals -- including for observables never measured, where a shot-based interval does not exist. Quality holds as the system grows (fidelity $0.90$ at $n{=}10$, gain \emph{growing} in $n$; $0.88$ at bond dimension $χ{=}4$), the parameterization is polynomial (native contraction to $20$ qubits), and we close the loop on IBM hardware ($5$ states at $0.97$ from hardware-measured Paulis).

</details>


### [99] [Barium-based Rydberg atom quantum technologies with long Rydberg coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11282)
*Xiao-Feng Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过钡原子的双光子激发方案，利用极小的双光子波矢消除多普勒限制的退相干，获得具有强偶极-偶极相互作用和长辐射寿命的6sng ^1G_4里德伯态，可促进中性原子量子计算和长寿命里德伯极化激元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光激发的里德伯态的相干时间通常受多普勒效应限制，远短于其本征寿命，这严重阻碍了里德伯介导的量子技术发展。

Method: 从钡原子的长寿命d轨道钟态出发，采用649 nm和658 nm的双光子激发方案，实现对6sng ^1G_4里德伯态的激发，该方案的双光子波矢极小，有效消除了多普勒限制的退相干。

Result: 该6sng ^1G_4里德伯态由于与邻近里德伯态之间存在较小的Förster缺陷，具有强偶极-偶极相互作用，同时拥有长辐射寿命。

Conclusion: 该方案可提升基于单个囚禁中性原子的量子计算性能，并在原子介质中实现长寿命里德伯极化激元，为全光量子信息处理带来新机遇。

Abstract: The short Doppler-limited coherence time of the laser-excited Rydberg state, usually orders of magnitude shorter than the lifetime of the Rydberg state, hinders the Rydberg-mediated quantum technologies. Here, we show that a 649~nm~$-$~658~nm two-photon excitation of the $6sng~^1G_4$ Rydberg state from a long-lived d-orbital clock state of barium can be achieved with a two-photon wavevector that is tiny, which effectively removes the Doppler-limited decoherence. Moreover, the $6sng~^1G_4$ Rydberg state has strong dipole-dipole interaction due to small Förster defect with nearby Rydberg states and possesses long radiative lifetime. These can benefit quantum computing based on individually trapped neutral atoms, and can enable long-lived Rydberg polaritons in atomic media, which brings fresh opportunities in all-optical quantum information.

</details>


### [100] [Experimental Observation of Anomalous Complementary Weak Values from Correlated Pairwise Two-State Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11284)
*Qian Xie,Liang Xu,Lijian Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用量子纠缠和后选择控制操作生成关联的成对双态矢量，将后选择从被动过滤转变为主动工程，从而在互斥的后选择分支中观测到异常的互补弱值，克服了传统弱值生成依赖近正交前后选择导致低成功概率的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常弱值的生成依赖于近正交的前后选择，这将其限制在单一后选择结果上，且成功概率极低，限制了实验可及性和时间对称性的充分应用。

Method: 利用量子纠缠和后选择控制操作，生成关联的成对双态矢量，将后选择从被动过滤转变为主动工程。

Result: 实现了在互斥的后选择分支中观测到异常的互补弱值，扩展了时间对称量子结构的操作可及性。

Conclusion: 该研究为探索时间对称性在量子信息处理中的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Weak values (WVs) arise from weak measurements performed within a time-symmetric formulation of quantum mechanics, where a system is both pre- and post-selected. Anomalous WVs that lie far outside the eigenvalue spectrum of the observable hold both fundamental and practical significance. However, their generation typically relies on near-orthogonal pre- and post-selection, which confines them to a single post-selection outcome with extremely low success probability. This constraint limits experimental accessibility and hinders the full exploitation of time symmetry. To overcome these limitations, we utilize quantum entanglement and post-selection-controlled operations to generate correlated pairwise two-state vectors. By changing the role of post-selection from passive filtering to active engineering, this approach enables the observation of anomalous complementary WVs associated with mutually exclusive post-selection branches. Our results extend the operational accessibility of time-symmetric quantum structures associated with the two-state vector formalism, and open new avenues for exploring the applications of time symmetry in quantum information processing.

</details>


### [101] [Information geometric quantification of effective privacy in quantum metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11302)
*Luca Bianchi,Shimpei Yamaguchi,Wojciech Roga,Davide Bacco,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种信息几何框架，通过参数空间中的体积测量来量化量子计量协议中参数的隐私性与可访问性，该框架统一了基于量子Fisher信息简并度的隐私定义，并使用扩展GHZ态展示了隐私与可访问性随量子关联和精度的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于量子Fisher信息简并度的隐私定义无法自然涵盖非完美实现（如有限精度测量），需要一种更通用的量化框架来有效刻画量子网络中参数的隐私性和可访问性。

Method: 提出信息几何框架：通过量子态诱导的参数空间中的体积测量来定义隐私和可访问性。以扩展GHZ态作为量子网络场景的代表性示例进行分析。

Result: 隐私和可访问性由量子关联和有限精度（测量精度）共同表征，推导出依赖于非完美测量和纠缠的标度律。

Conclusion: 该框架统一并推广了基于量子Fisher信息简并度的隐私定义，能自然处理非完美实现，为量子计量协议中参数隐私性的量化提供了更通用的工具。

Abstract: Privacy of a quantum metrological protocol concerns the extent to which single parameters can be kept inaccessible to an observer or to other users of the network. In this work, an information geometric framework is developed to quantify privacy and accessibility of functions of parameters effectively, that is, up to a finite accuracy in state discrimination. Both quantities are defined by measuring volumes in the parameter space induced by the underlying quantum states. This construction subsumes previous definitions of privacy based on the degeneracy of quantum Fisher information, naturally encompassing imperfect implementations. Using extended-GHZ states as a representative example of a quantum network scenario, privacy and accessibility are characterized by quantum correlations and accuracy, providing scaling laws depending on imperfect measurements and entanglement.

</details>


### [102] [Controlling the Inhomogeneous Broadening and Impedance Matching of a Spin Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11315)
*Mathieu Couillard,Debdip Guchait,Hiroki Takahashi,Yuimaru Kubo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过反亥姆霍兹线圈在稀释制冷机中施加磁场梯度控制自旋系综的频谱分布，实现了自旋系综与传输线之间的阻抗匹配（-50 dB吸收），并实验演示了从弱耦合到强耦合的可调谐转变，朝着基于自旋系综的微波光子量子存储器迈出了第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现基于自旋系综的量子存储器，需要高效耦合自旋系综与微波光子，并控制耦合强度从弱到强的过渡。

Method: 利用反亥姆霍兹线圈在稀释制冷机中施加磁场梯度，调控自旋系综的频谱分布；通过阻抗匹配实现传输线与自旋系综的高效耦合；结合连续波和时域脉冲测量系统调控弱-强耦合转变。

Result: 实现了-50 dB的吸收（阻抗匹配），成功在连续波和时域脉冲测量中可调谐地跨越弱耦合到强耦合的转变。

Conclusion: 这项工作为基于自旋系综的微波光子量子存储器奠定了基础，展示了利用磁场梯度精确控制自旋-谐振器耦合的动力学的可行性。

Abstract: We control the spectral distribution of a spin ensemble by applying a magnetic field gradient using an anti-Helmholtz coil inside a dilution refrigerator, and demonstrate impedance matching between the ensemble and a transmission line, achieving -50 dB absorption of incident radiation. This represents a first step toward a spin-ensemble-based quantum memory for itinerant microwave photons. We further model the spectral distribution under the applied gradient to predict the spin-resonator response, and use the device to systematically tune the weak-to-strong coupling transition in both continuous-wave and time-domain pulsed measurements.

</details>


### [103] [Moment-based PPT criteria for random bipartite states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11369)
*Cécilia Lancien,Kieran McShane*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一种基于矩的PPT准则层级结构在高维两体系统中的典型检测表现，发现了随机混合态满足或违反这些准则的阈值环境维度，并利用组合数学、测度集中和正交多项式等工具进行了理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩基PPT松弛准则作为仅涉及可实验观测量的纠缠判据层级已被提出，但其在高维两体系统中的典型检测性能尚不清楚，本文旨在系统研究其普适性表现。

Method: 通过组合置换技术估计部分转置随机态矩的平均值，结合测度集中论证约束偏差概率，并利用Hankel行列式评估及正交多项式理论进行阈值分析。

Result: 对每个固定层级m，识别出环境维度阈值s=λ_m d²，在此阈值下随机态从违反变为满足PPT准则，且该行为随d增大以概率1发生。

Conclusion: 得出了高阶矩基PPT准则在高维随机两体混合态中检测性能的严格阈值，揭示了噪声维度与纠缠检测能力之间的定量关系。

Abstract: Moment-based relaxations of the positive partial transpose (PPT) criterion have been recently introduced, as a hierarchy of entanglement criteria involving only experimentally accessible quantities of a given bipartite state. The goal of this work is to study their typical detection performance on high-dimensional bipartite systems. Concretely, we investigate whether random bipartite mixed states on $\mathbb C^d\otimes\mathbb C^d$, obtained as the marginal over an environment $\mathbb C^s$ of a uniformly distributed pure state, generically satisfy or violate them. For each fixed level $m\in\mathbb N$ in this hierarchy of moment-based PPT criteria, we are able to identify a threshold environment dimension $s=λ_md^2$ at which the behavior of the associated random state switches from violating to satisfying it, with probability going to $1$ as $d$ grows. The proof combines combinatorics of permutations techniques to estimate the average value of moments of partially transposed random states and concentration of measure arguments to bound the probability of deviating from such average, when the underlying local dimension $d$ is large. We additionally need tools from the theory of Hankel determinant evaluation via orthogonal polynomials.

</details>


### [104] [Symmetry tests for cyclic groups with quantum linear optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11393)
*Carlos Navas-Merlo,Juan Carlos García Escartín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种检测光子态在循环群作用下是否对称的方法，推广了圆测试和SWAP测试，可用于傅里叶干涉仪的抑制律、二进制移位排列测试、多光子态本征态搜索，并讨论了其在Hadamard测试中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态对称性检测在量子态比较及更复杂量子算法中具有多种应用，现有方法（如圆测试、SWAP测试）有待推广到更广泛的循环群对称性场景。

Method: 提出一种方法，通过构建以根为单位特征值的算子$\hat{S}$来定义循环群，检测输入光子态在该群作用下的不变性。

Result: 推广了已知的圆测试和SWAP测试，将其与傅里叶干涉仪中的抑制律相关联；提供了基于二进制移位的排列新测试方法以及多光子态中本征态的搜索方式。

Conclusion: 该方法可进一步用于实现Hadamard测试，这是变分量子算法和量子机器学习中的基本原语。

Abstract: Testing quantum states for symmetries has multiple applications in quantum state comparison or as a primitive in more complex quantum algorithms. We present a method that determines whether an input photonic state is invariant under the action of a cyclic group defined by an operator $\hat{S}$ with eigenvalues which are roots of unity. The results generalize previously known circle and SWAP tests related to suppression laws in Fourier interferometers and offer a new test for permutations defined by a binary shift and a way to search for eigenstates in multiphoton states. Finally, we discuss the scenarios where this measurement can be used to perform Hadamard test, a fundamental primitive in variational and quantum machine-learning algorithms.

</details>


### [105] [From Leaves to Clusters: Depth-Efficient SAT-Oracle Synthesis Based on the HRSE Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11401)
*Zhihang Li,Wei Zi,Shuai Yang,Bei Zhou,Zongjiang Yi,Woji He,Yingjie Lin,Kaixiang Ji,Heru Du,Jinchen Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Clustered Synthesis Tree (CST)框架，通过将子句聚类暴露实例相关的子句级并行性，在有限辅助量子比特预算下显著降低SAT(CNF)公式的量子Oracle电路深度，相较现有方法深度减少68%–94%，在标准基准上实现2.6×–43.2×的深度缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAT(CNF)量子Oracle合成方法在有限辅助量子比特预算下产生的电路深度较大，未能充分利用子句级并行性，而电路深度直接影响量子算法的整体性能。

Method: 提出CST框架，包含三个部分：(1) SeedGrow启发式算法（时间复杂度O(m² k)），解决子句分组问题（形式化为辅助量子比特约束调度问题，证明为NP完全）；(2) ClausePack可逆Oracle，以仅对数深度开销并行评估聚类内的子句；(3) CST-Map，将聚类树编译为可执行的SAT-Oracle。

Result: 在随机4-CNF上，相同辅助量子比特预算下深度降低68%–94%；在SATLIB基准上实现约2.6×–43.2×的深度缩减；达到基线最大预算深度仅需其3.7%–20%的辅助量子比特；Grover搜索资源评估显示总电路深度降低70%–89%。

Conclusion: CST框架通过挖掘子句级并行性，有效缓解了量子Oracle合成中深度与辅助量子比特预算之间的权衡，在广泛基准测试中显著优于现有方法，并将优势传递到完整Grover搜索算法中。

Abstract: Quantum oracles are a common building block of many quantum algorithms, where circuit depth is a primary cost that directly affects overall performance. Synthesizing oracles for SAT (CNF) formulas under a limited ancilla budget, however, tends to yield deep circuits, as existing methods underexploit clause-level parallelism. In this work, we present the Clustered Synthesis Tree (CST), a depth-oriented framework whose core idea is to group the individual clause leaves of a hierarchical synthesis tree into clusters, exposing instance-dependent clause-level parallelism under ancilla constraints. CST comprises three parts: the clause-grouping problem it induces, which we formulate as an ancilla-constrained scheduling problem and prove NP-complete in general, is addressed by SeedGrow, a polynomial-time $O(m^2 k)$ heuristic; ClausePack, a reversible oracle that evaluates a cluster's clauses in parallel at only a logarithmic-depth overhead; and CST-Map, which compiles the clustered tree into an executable SAT-oracle. On random $4$-CNF under the same ancilla budgets, CST reduces the oracle's circuit depth over the state-of-the-art (SOTA) baseline by $68\%$--$94\%$. On the standard SATLIB benchmarks, CST achieves about a $2.6\times$--$43.2\times$ reduction over the SOTA baseline, with the largest gains under dense variable sharing, and matches the baseline's maximum-budget depth using only $3.7\%$--$20\%$ of its ancilla qubits. A Grover-search resource estimate shows the advantage carries over to the full algorithm, reducing total circuit depth by $70\%$--$89\%$.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum algorithms for second-order boundary value problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11410)
*Lauri Kettunen,Tejas Shinde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于星形局部更新规则的框架，通过外微分、对偶算子及霍奇算子的离散化，显式构建与量子计算兼容的二阶微分算子（如散度-梯度、旋度-旋度），避免了传统矩阵表示，从而导出简单、统一且可扩展的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于矩阵的数值格式可能掩盖量子电路可利用的局部几何结构，本文旨在建立一种显式构造离散微分算子的框架，使其与量子计算天然兼容。

Method: 从外微分、其对偶算子和霍奇算子出发，在原始-对偶胞腔复形上推导二阶算子的离散实现，并将其重构为星形局部更新规则——通过显式函数返回相关边界链而非矩阵表示。

Result: 成功构建了散度-梯度算子和旋度-旋度算子的量子电路，证明同一星形局部编译原理可统一适用于不同算子、胞腔复形及流形维度。

Conclusion: 该框架为偏微分方程的量子算法提供了系统化构建路径，生成的量子电路简单、统一且具有可扩展性。

Abstract: Second-order boundary value problems are central to computational science, yet standard matrix-based numerical formulations can obscure the local geometric structure that quantum circuits may exploit. Here we introduce a framework for explicitly constructing finite-dimensional counterparts of continuous differential operators in a form compatible with quantum computation. Starting from the exterior derivative, its adjoint, and the Hodge operator, we derive discrete realizations of second-order operators on primal--dual cell complexes and reformulate them as star-local update rules expressed through explicit functions that return the relevant bounding chains rather than through matrix representations. This yields simple, uniform, and scalable quantum circuits. We demonstrate the construction for div--grad and curl--curl operators, showing that the same star-local compilation principle extends across different operators, cell complexes, and manifold dimensions within a common framework. More generally, the framework provides a systematic route to quantum algorithms for partial differential equations.

</details>


### [107] [Strong Zero Modes in Supersymmetry-Inspired Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11483)
*Alberto Zorzato,Pietro Richelli,Jiri Minář,Kareljan Schoutens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究超对称1+1D量子场论S-矩阵对应的量子电路中的离散动力学，发现砖墙结构中存在局域化和弹道传播的强零模（SZM），并探讨了利用传播SZM进行量子信息传输及其对噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究超对称量子场论离散化后的电路中强零模的存在性及其动力学行为，特别是SZM能否从边界传播到体态，以及如何利用其进行量子信息传输。

Method: 采用砖墙构型的量子电路，其量子门对应于超对称1+1D量子场论的S-矩阵，通过分析SZM的局域化与弹道传播行为，并引入强Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用解释传播机制。

Result: 发现SZM的数量依赖于参数区间；部分SZM保持边界局域化，而另一些则沿弹道轨迹传播；传播行为可由强DM相互作用解释。

Conclusion: 传播SZM可用于量子信息传输，且对多种噪声具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate discrete dynamics in quantum circuits with gates corresponding to the S-matrix of a supersymmetric 1+1D quantum field theory. We show that for a brick-wall configuration such circuits support both localized and delocalized dynamically conserved operators known as strong zero modes (SZM), the number of which depends on the parameter regime. We demonstrate that, while some of the SZM remain localized at boundaries, other SZM propagate ballistically, guided by a choice of circuit parameters. Such propagation can be explained by a strong Dzyaloshinskii Moriya term appearing in the dynamics. We describe how to exploit propagating SZM for quantum information transport and discuss the robustness to various types of noise.

</details>


### [108] [AtomFlow: An End-to-End FPGA-Based Control Architecture for Neutral Atom Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11490)
*Xiaorang Guo,Jonas Winklmann,Vengkeat Chea,Martin Schulz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: AtomFlow是一种基于FPGA的控制架构，通过将荧光图像分析和原子重排算法整合到单个Zynq UltraScale+设备上，以流式方式发出重排移动指令，将端到端延迟降低到25.3 ms，解决了中性原子量子计算中经典处理环节导致的执行速度慢的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子计算（NAQC）虽有长相干时间和原子自然相同等优势，但执行速度慢，瓶颈在于荧光成像、冷却和原子重排等长时间经典处理任务。

Method: 提出AtomFlow架构，将荧光图像分析和新开发的原子重排算法整合到单个Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA上，两个阶段同板部署，重排移动计算完成后以流式方式立即发出，消除传统主机中介管线的往返延迟。

Result: 在16x16原子阵列上，AtomFlow实现端到端延迟25.3 ms，首次移动延迟4 ms，平均移动生成时间1 ms；可扩展性分析表明该架构可在单板资源预算内支持更大原子阵列。

Conclusion: AtomFlow通过FPGA上的流水线协同部署有效消除了NAQC中经典处理环节的延迟瓶颈，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Neutral Atom Quantum Computing (NAQC) is an emerging modality for scalable quantum computation, valued for its long coherence times and the naturally identical atomic qubits. However, one of the main drawbacks is its slow execution rate, dominated by lengthy classical processing tasks, such as fluorescence imaging, cooling, and atom rearrangement. We address this bottleneck with AtomFlow, a field-programmable gate array (FPGA)-based control architecture that consolidates fluorescence-image analysis and a newly developed atom-rearrangement algorithm onto a single Zynq UltraScale+ device. By co-locating the two stages on the same board and emitting rearrangement moves in a streaming fashion as soon as they are computed, AtomFlow eliminates the round-trip latency of conventional host-mediated pipelines. Evaluated on a 16x16 atom array, AtomFlow achieves an end-to-end latency of 25.3 ms with a first-move latency of 4 ms and an average move generation of 1 ms. Furthermore, our scalability analysis demonstrates that the architecture can readily support larger atom arrays within a single-board resource budget.

</details>


### [109] [A diode nanocavity for fast, efficient and tunable emission of highly entangled photon pairs and Fourier-transform-limited single photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11494)
*Ievgen Brytavskyi,Thomas Oberleitner,Christian Weidinger,Maximilian Aigner,Gabriel Undeutsch,Tobias Steindl,Johannes Reindl,Ailton Garcia,Melina Peter,Christian Schimpf,Santanu Manna,Michele B. Rota,Quirin Buchinger,Sven Höfling,Tobias Huber-Loyola,Rinaldo Trotta,Tobias M. Krieger,Eva Schöll,Armando Rastelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于量子点嵌入p-i-n二极管圆形布拉格光栅谐振器的可调谐纳米光电器件，实现了高达0.55(6)的提取效率和~8的Purcell因子，能够产生波长可调谐的纠缠光子对（原始并发度>0.89）以及高度不可区分的单光子（原始HOM可见度0.951(4)），为半导体量子光子学提供了可行平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于半导体量子点的纠缠光子源在提取效率、Purcell增强和电荷噪声（导致闪烁并降低不可区分性）之间存在权衡，需要一种能够同时优化这些指标的新型器件架构。

Method: 将量子点嵌入p-i-n二极管结构的圆形布拉格光栅谐振器中，利用电控调谐实现波长可调，抑制电荷噪声引起的闪烁现象。

Result: 提取效率达0.55(6)，Purcell因子~8；在1.6 nm范围内产生波长可调谐纠缠光子对，原始（校正）并发度>0.89（0.91）；单光子原始（校正）HOM可见度为0.951(4)（0.988(6)），接近傅里叶极限。

Conclusion: 该器件验证了同时实现高效纠缠光子对产生和高度不可区分单光子发射的可行性，为半导体量子光子学提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Deterministic sources of entangled photon pairs and indistinguishable photons are expected to play a key role in photonic quantum technologies. Semiconductor quantum dots are promising candidates due to their on-demand emission and compatibility with nanophotonic structures. However, current implementations face trade-offs between extraction efficiency, Purcell enhancement, as well as charge noise that causes blinking and degrades indistinguishability. Here we demonstrate a tunable nano-optoelectronic device based on a quantum dot embedded in a p-i-n diode circular-Bragg-grating-resonator and featuring extraction efficiencies up to 0.55(6) and Purcell-factor of $\sim$8. The device generates wavelength-tunable entangled photon pairs with suppressed blinking and raw (corrected) concurrence > 0.89 (0.91) over a range of 1.6 nm. The very same source also emits single, nearly Fourier-limited and highly indistinguishable photons with raw (corrected) $\mathcal{V}_{\text{HOM}}$ = 0.951(4) (0.988(6)). These results demonstrate a viable platform for semiconductor quantum photonics.

</details>


### [110] [States dressing analysis in a transmon-transmon-bus system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11514)
*P. A. Gladilovich,R. A. Migdisov,A. V. Sabluk,P. S. Burtsev,N. N. Abramov,N. Y. Rudenko,V. I. Chichkov,N. A. Maleeva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验和理论分析了超导量子处理器中transmon-transmon-bus系统的计算态修饰效应，通过三种方法（全三元素模型、有效模方法、未扰动模方法）评估修饰程度，并展示了可调谐修饰范围对优化多量子比特门保真度的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中，非计算态对计算态的修饰会限制多量子比特门的保真度，因此需要准确估计和控制这种修饰效应以优化门操作。

Method: 在transmon-transmon-bus系统中，实验和解析地研究了计算态修饰的可调谐性；采用三种方法（全三元素模型、有效模方法、未扰动模方法）估计修饰程度。

Result: 三种方法得到的修饰估计结果揭示了准确估计计算态修饰的重要性，并展示了该系统可在较宽范围内调控修饰效应。

Conclusion: 准确估计和控制计算态修饰对于在超导平台上优化门操作至关重要。

Abstract: The multi-qubit gates fidelity of superconducting quantum processors can be limited due to the dressing of computational states by noncomputational ones. Here, we experimentally and analytically investigate a transmon-transmon-bus system where the computational states dressing is tunable over a broad range. We estimate the dressing using three methods: a full three-element model, an effective mode approach, and an unperturbed mode approach. The obtained results highlight the importance of the accurate estimation and control of the computational states dressing in order to optimize gates on superconducting platform.

</details>


### [111] [Moment-Structured Block Encodings of Periodic Finite-Difference Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11596)
*Jishnu Mahmud,Rebekah Herrman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个框架，用于在周期性网格上对平移不变的有限差分算子进行块编码（block encoding），通过分析模板的矩阶数，统一确定了连续算子、傅里叶符号的消失阶数以及块编码的成本，并推导出了最优次归一化（optimal subnormalization）的闭式判据。该框架推广了拉普拉斯算子的最优构造，并首次给出了平流-扩散族算子的显式空间块编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块编码是量子线性代数算法中访问矩阵数据的标准技术，其实现直接影响算法的成功概率、模拟时间和下游成本。然而，仅有少数算子具有显式且次归一化最优的块编码构造，且设计过程依赖定制化计算。本文旨在为科学计算中核心的有限差分算子（平移不变、常系数PDE的离散化）提供系统化的最优块编码框架。

Method: 利用有限差分模板的矩阶数（moment order）来同时确定：所近似的连续算子、傅里叶符号的消失阶数（vanishing order）以及块编码的成本。基于此，推导出关于模板系数的闭式最优性判据，用于判定构造是否在整个算子族（均匀网格尺寸）上达到最优次归一化，并在未达到时量化差距。

Result: 该框架涵盖了文献中拉普拉斯算子的最优块编码构造，并能验证更高偶数阶的新实例（包括双调和算子）。此外，推导了由算子符号谱性质参数化的成功概率下界，并给出了平流-扩散族算子的显式常数——该族此前尚无显式空间块编码。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一且可证明最优的块编码框架，适用于平移不变有限差分算子，降低了定制化设计的门槛，并拓展了量子算法在科学计算中的应用范围。

Abstract: Block encoding is the standard technique for accessing matrix data in quantum linear-algebra algorithms. Its implementation directly affects its subnormalization, which in turn controls the algorithm's success probability, simulation time, and downstream costs. Explicit construction of block encodings with provably optimal subnormalization exists for only a handful of operators, with bespoke calculations used in its design. In this work, we develop a framework to block encode translation-invariant finite-difference operators on a periodic grid. These operators are the finite-difference discretizations of the constant-coefficient partial differential equations that sit at the core of scientific computing. We show that the moment order of these stencils can be used to simultaneously determine the continuum operator approximated, the vanishing order of the Fourier symbol, and the cost of the block encoding. From there, we derive a closed-form optimality criterion as a function of the stencil coefficients, which certifies whether the construction attains the optimal subnormalization for an entire operator family, uniformly in grid size, and quantifies the gap when it does not. The framework subsumes optimal constructions for the Laplacian operator in the literature and can be used to certify new instances at higher even orders, including the biharmonic operator. Furthermore, we derive success-probability floors parameterized by spectral properties of the operator's symbol and find explicit constants for the block encoding of the advection-diffusion family for which no prior explicit spatial block encoding exists.

</details>


### [112] [From Circuits to Hardware: Benchmarking Standard and Qubit-Efficient Quantum Optimization on Real Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11637)
*Monit Sharma,Hoong Chuin Lau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在IBM Heron量子处理器上对四种组合优化问题（MDKP、MIS、QAP、MSP）进行了全面的门级量子优化基准测试，涵盖VQE、CVaR-VQE、标准/多角度/温启动QAOA以及量子比特高效方法（PCE、QRAO）。研究发现，当双量子比特门数约770（F_est≈0.1）时噪声主导执行开始显现；QAP因密集编码和极稀疏可行流形导致所有硬件方法均无法产生可行解；QAOA系列编译后电路普遍受噪声主导；量子比特高效方法虽能扩展实例规模，但受限于保真度预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子优化研究进展迅速，但缺乏在统一协议下对多种算法族进行跨不同组合问题的真实硬件基准测试。现有研究多聚焦单一算法或单一问题，缺乏系统性、多问题、多算法的公平比较，无法全面评估当前门级量子计算在组合优化上的实际能力与局限性。

Method: 在IBM Heron r1/r2处理器上，使用二级弹性纠错缓解策略，对四种NP难问题（MDKP、MIS、QAP、MSP）测试了VQE、CVaR-VQE、标准/多角度/温启动QAOA，以及量子比特高效方法PCE和QRAO。报告了247种方法-实例组合的编译后电路大小、硬件结果和基于独立误差门计数的保真度代理F_est。包含首个QRAO真实硬件结果和首个多问题PCE硬件基准。

Result: 1) 对于MDKP和MIS，F_est≈0.1（对应约770个双量子比特门）是噪声主导执行的阈值；2) QAP在n=10时可行分数为10!/2^100，所有硬件方法均无法产生可行解；3) QAOA族编译后电路普遍噪声主导，除一个有限样本MIS温启动例外，大多数低保真度可行结果与均匀随机控制范围重叠；4) SWAP感知的分数门Nighthawk拓扑编译对比可减少双量子比特门数，但所有电路F_est<10^-3；5) 量子比特高效方法可扩展可运行实例规模，但受限于经验保真度预算。

Conclusion: 当前门级量子处理器在组合优化上的实际能力受到噪声和编译开销的严格限制。QAOA族在现有硬件条件下普遍噪声主导，QAP类稠密编码问题对当前处理器尤为困难。量子比特高效方法虽能缓解部分限制，但保真度预算仍是核心瓶颈。结论局限于特定实现而非QAOA整体，提示未来需更有效的编译优化和误差缓解技术。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in quantum optimization, broad real-hardware benchmarks comparing multiple algorithmic families across diverse combinatorial problems under a common protocol remain limited. We benchmark gate-based quantum optimization on four NP-hard problems: multi-dimensional knapsack (MDKP), maximum independent set (MIS), quadratic assignment (QAP), and market-share (MSP). We study VQE, CVaR-VQE, standard, multi-angle, and warm-start QAOA, together with qubit-efficient PCE and QRAO, on IBM Heron r1/r2 processors using resilience-level-2 mitigation. To our knowledge, this includes the first real-hardware QRAO results and the first multi-problem PCE hardware benchmark.
  Across 247 method-instance combinations, we report transpiled circuit size, hardware outcomes, and an independent-error gate-count fidelity proxy, $F_{\mathrm{est}}$. For MDKP and MIS, an empirical operating point near $F_{\mathrm{est}}\approx 0.1$, corresponding to about 770 two-qubit gates at the median Heron-r2 CZ error rate, marks the onset of noise-dominated execution. QAP exposes a separate bottleneck: dense one-hot encodings and an exponentially sparse feasible manifold, with feasible fraction $10!/2^{100}$ at $n=10$; no tested hardware method produces a feasible assignment. Compiled QAOA-family circuits are generally noise dominated, and a matched uniform-random control shows that most feasible low-fidelity outcomes fall within the random range, apart from one finite-sample MIS warm-start exception. A SWAP-aware, fractional-gate, Nighthawk-topology compilation counterfactual reduces two-qubit counts but leaves all circuits below $F_{\mathrm{est}}=10^{-3}$. These conclusions apply to the tested implementations rather than QAOA in general. Qubit-efficient methods extend runnable instance sizes, but only within the empirical fidelity budget.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-Stage Mamba-Based Architecture for Fast and Scalable Superconducting Qubit Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11668)
*Luca Otting,Xiaorang Guo,Emmanouil Giortamis,Benjamin Lienhard,Pramod Bhatotia,Martin Schulz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Mamba模型的多阶段量子比特状态判别器，通过线性复杂度的序列建模实现高效读出。轻量模型在几何平均读出保真度达0.906，参数减少49.6%；最优模型达0.911。在500ns短读出时长下仍保持0.893保真度，逻辑错误率较此前最优方法降低26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器中，读出操作既易出错又高延迟，尤其在频分复用架构中信号串扰严重降低读出保真度。现有基于前馈神经网络的ML方法参数规模大，且缺乏联合处理弛豫误差和判别量子态的端到端网络。

Method: 采用Mamba模型实现线性复杂度的高效序列建模，构建多阶段量子比特状态判别器：第一阶段进行初始状态判别，第二阶段识别并缓解弛豫引起的误差。模型经过轻量化设计。

Result: 轻量模型几何平均读出保真度0.906，参数减少49.6%；最优模型保真度0.911。在500ns短读出时长下保真度达0.893，逻辑错误率较此前最优方法降低26%。

Conclusion: 基于Mamba的多阶段判别器在参数效率、读出保真度和鲁棒性方面均超越现有方法，为容错量子计算中的读出瓶颈提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable qubit readout is a critical bottleneck toward fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC). In superconducting quantum processors, readout operations are both error-prone and high-latency. These challenges become more severe in frequency-multiplexed architectures, where signal crosstalk among neighboring qubits significantly degrades readout fidelity. Existing machine learning (ML)-based approaches rely on feed-forward neural networks (FNNs) that suffer from large parameter sizes and lack an end-to-end network that jointly addresses relaxation errors and discriminates qubit states.
  In this work, we present a multi-stage qubit state discriminator based on the Mamba model, which enables efficient sequence modeling with linear complexity. The first stage performs initial state discrimination, followed by a refinement stage that identifies and mitigates relaxation-induced errors. Our lightweight model achieves a geometric mean readout fidelity of 0.906, outperforming the best-reported state-of-the-art method while reducing parameter size by 49.6%; our optimal model further reaches 0.911. Both models remain robust across varying input trace lengths, maintaining a high fidelity of 0.893 at readout durations as short as 500 $ns$, achieving up to a 26% reduction in logical error rate over prior work in quantum error correction (QEC).

</details>


### [114] [$\mathtt{Q^2SAR}$: overcoming classical bottlenecks in drug discovery via quantum multiple kernel learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11701)
*Mariano Caruso,Daniel Ruiz,Alejandro Giraldo,Guido Bellomo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Next-Gen Q²SAR的量子多核学习（QMKL）框架，利用量子支持向量机（QSVMs）增强药物发现中的定量构效关系（QSAR）建模。在DYRK1A激酶数据集上，QMKL-SVM的AUC达到0.8750，显著优于经典梯度提升模型的0.8037，并建立了通过投影量子核（PQK）解决经典数据瓶颈的理论与实证路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典QSAR方法难以有效捕捉分子数据中高度复杂、非线性和高维的相互作用，导致预测精度下降和后期临床失败率高。为解决这一瓶颈，作者引入量子计算增强建模的表达能力。

Method: 提出QMKL框架，将分子描述符编码到指数级量子希尔伯特空间中，利用QSVM进行非线性建模，并引入投影量子核（PQK）和测量加速器以缓解经典数据瓶颈。

Result: 在DYRK1A激酶数据集上，QMKL-SVM的AUC为0.8750，显著优于经典梯度提升模型的0.8037。

Conclusion: 随着量子计算架构的成熟，该框架有望推动自主认知架构和自我改进的药物发现管线的发展，加速生命拯救性治疗药物的开发。

Abstract: Quantitative Structure-Activity Relationship ($\mathtt{QSAR}$) modeling is a foundational computational methodology in early-stage drug discovery, heavily relied upon for predicting compound toxicity, bioavailability, and therapeutic potential. However, classical methods often struggle to effectively map the highly complex, non-linear, and high-dimensional interactions inherent in molecular data, leading to reduced predictive accuracy and costly late-stage clinical failures. In this paper, we present a Quantum Multiple Kernel Learning ($\mathtt{QMKL}$) framework, dubbed Next-Gen $\mathtt{Q^2SAR}$, that leverages Quantum Support Vector Machines ($\mathtt{QSVMs}$) to overcome these classical limitations. By encoding molecular descriptors into exponentially large quantum Hilbert spaces, our approach substantially enhances the expressiveness of non-linear modeling. Benchmarking our quantum-enhanced framework on a dataset targeting the $\mathtt{DYRK1A}$ kinase (a critical target for Alzheimer's disease), the $\mathtt{QMKL}$-$\mathtt{SVM}$ achieves an impressive Area Under the Curve ($\mathtt{AUC}$) score of $0.8750$, significantly outperforming classical state-of-the-art Gradient Boosting models ($\mathtt{AUC} = 0.8037$). Furthermore, we establish a theoretical and empirical pathway toward resolving classical data bottlenecks through projected quantum kernels ($\mathtt{PQK}$) and measurement accelerators. As quantum computing architecture matures, this framework paves the way for autonomous cognitive architectures and self-improving drug discovery pipelines, promising to unlock deeper insights across vast chemical spaces and to accelerate the development of life-saving therapeutics.

</details>


### [115] [Near-Optimal Mode Scaling for Finite-Dimensional Boson Sampling via Lie-Algebraic Leakage Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11708)
*Chon-Fai Kam,En-Jui Kuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对基于确定性物质平台的玻色采样中因局域希尔伯特空间截断导致的聚束泄露问题，建立了基于紧李群不可约表示的统一框架，并证明了在Gaussian矩阵模型下泄露算子的谱范数以$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$集中，而非先前自旋模拟的$O(n)$最坏情况；通过Haar系综验证，将所需模式数从$m=\Omega(n^4)$压缩至近最优的$m=\tilde{\Omega}(n^{1+2/(d-1)})$，对自旋-1表示（$d=3$）开销降至$m=\tilde{\Omega}(n^2)$，与无碰撞阈值匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在确定性物质平台上实现玻色采样时，玻色模需编码在有限维局域希尔伯特空间（截断维度$d$）中，这引入了线性光学中不存在的泄露通道——多粒子超出局部截断的聚束效应。需要量化这种泄露对采样硬度的资源开销影响。

Method: 建立了基于紧李群不可约表示的非相互作用采样统一框架，其中跃迁振幅为单粒子跃迁矩阵子矩阵的immanant；通过Dyson级数分析将关联多体泄露算符分解为独立随机矩阵，应用非交换浓度不等式证明谱范数集中性；通过Haar系综的次矩阵比较输入验证。

Result: 在Gaussian模型下泄露算符谱范数以$\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$集中（而非$O(n)$）；Haar系综匹配闭式$\sqrt{d(n-d+1)}$，精度达亚百分比；所需模式数从$m=\Omega(n^4)$降至$m=\tilde{\Omega}(n^{1+2/(d-1)})$，$d=3$时$m=\tilde{\Omega}(n^2)$。

Conclusion: 结果与粒子统计无关，适用于有限维李对称体系结构，量化了维持采样硬度所需的空间资源；证明了物理Haar系综与Gaussian模型间的差距可简化为单一子矩阵比较问题。

Abstract: Boson sampling demonstrates quantum advantage through the interference of indistinguishable particles, with output probabilities governed by matrix permanents. Realizing it on deterministic, matter-based platforms requires encoding the bosonic modes in finite-dimensional local Hilbert spaces, which introduces a leakage channel absent in linear optics: multi-particle bunching beyond the local truncation $d$. We develop a unified framework for non-interacting sampling on the irreducible representations of compact Lie groups, in which the transition amplitude is the immanant of a submatrix of the single-particle transition matrix, recovering the permanent in the bosonic case. Within this framework we bound the bunching leakage through a Dyson-series analysis: decomposing the correlated many-body leakage operator into independent random matrices and applying non-commutative concentration inequalities, we prove, in a Gaussian model of the transition matrix, that its spectral norm concentrates at $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{n})$ rather than the $O(n)$ worst-case of prior spin-based emulations; the passage to the physical Haar ensemble is reduced to a single submatrix-comparison input, verified at leading order. Exact numerics across local dimensions $d=2$--$5$ indicate that the bound is tight, the Haar-ensemble norm matching the closed form $\sqrt{d(n-d+1)}$ to sub-percent accuracy. This tightens the required mode number from $m=Ω(n^4)$ to the near-optimal $m=\tildeΩ(n^{1+2/(d-1)})$; for a spin-1 representation ($d=3$) the overhead falls to $m=\tildeΩ(n^2)$, matching the collision-free threshold. The result is independent of particle statistics and applies across finite-dimensional Lie-symmetric architectures, quantifying the spatial resources needed to preserve sampling hardness.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Arithmetic Circuits in Public-Key Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11713)
*Siyi Wang,Kyungbae Jang,Hyunji Kim,Anik Basu Bhaumik,Anubhab Baksi,Hwajeong Seo,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子算术电路在公钥密码分析中的设计与优化策略，重点讨论了基于测量的逆计算和条件干净辅助位等技术，并回顾了加法、乘法、模幂运算等核心算术操作的最新设计方案，以及容错运行时和资源估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算术电路是量子算法（尤其是密码分析算法）的基础构建模块，但其设计面临不可克隆定理、量子比特限制和电路深度约束等挑战，亟需资源高效的优化策略以实现大规模量子计算的实际应用。

Method: 通过综述方式梳理了量子算术电路的关键优化技术（如测量-based uncomputation和conditionally clean ancilla），回顾了加法、乘法、模幂运算等核心操作的最新设计，并介绍了容错量子密码分析中的运行时与资源估计方法。

Result: 系统总结了当前量子算术电路在公钥密码分析领域的最先进设计方案及其资源优化策略，为量化评估量子密码分析能力提供了基础。

Conclusion: 本文强调资源高效型量子算术电路设计的重要性，为现实评估量子密码分析能力奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Quantum computing has advanced rapidly in recent decades, driven by developments across the technology stack, including quantum error-correcting codes and efficient quantum algorithms. Among these, quantum arithmetic circuits serve as fundamental building blocks for various promising algorithms. Despite their crucial role, the design of quantum arithmetic circuits faces challenges arising from the no-cloning theorem, qubit limitations, and circuit depth constraints, which significantly impact the efficiency of large-scale quantum computing. We provide an overview of quantum arithmetic circuits in the context of public-key cryptanalysis, with particular emphasis on optimization strategies such as measurement-based uncomputation and conditionally clean ancilla. We review state-of-the-art designs for essential arithmetic operations in public-key cryptanalysis such as addition, multiplication, and modular exponentiation. We also present an overview of the techniques used for fault-tolerant runtime and resource estimation in quantum cryptanalysis. In brief, this chapter emphasizes strategies for designing resource-efficient quantum arithmetic circuits, providing a basis for realistic evaluations of quantum cryptanalytic capabilities.

</details>


### [117] [Interlocked Time Crystal in Coupled Spin-1/2 Ensembles under Local Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11717)
*Zhen-Huan Yang,Zhen-Tao Liang,Dan-Bo Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过耦合两个简单的自旋-1/2 ensembles（每个都不单独支持时间晶体），可以合成一个复合耗散时间晶体，其动力学特征类似于多能级系统，但不需要扩大局部希尔伯特空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在不扩大局部希尔伯特空间的前提下，通过耦合简单两能级子系统来生成耗散时间晶体的机制。

Method: 耦合两个局域泵浦和衰减的自旋-1/2 ensembles，利用相反的泵浦-衰减不平衡和ensemble间交换耦合；通过平均场分析、有限尺寸下Liouvillian谱精确计算、累积量展开的时间关联分析进行验证。

Result: 两个单独的ensemble均不支持自主振荡相，但耦合后形成一个具有固定内相位关系的连锁时间晶体，且不存在于任何单个ensemble中。

Conclusion: 耦合简单两能级子系统可以生成耗散时间晶体序，提供了一种构建有效内部结构的途径，替代了多能级组分的需求。

Abstract: Multilevel dissipative systems can exploit multiple local transitions and coherence channels to generate nonstationary time-crystalline dynamics. Here we show that an analogous mechanism can be synthesized without enlarging the local Hilbert space by coupling two locally pumped and decaying spin-1/2 ensembles into a composite dissipative unit.Neither ensemble supports an autonomous oscillatory phase; instead, opposite pump-decay imbalances and inter-ensemble exchange coupling can lead to a single interlocked time crystal with a fixed internal phase relation and no single-ensemble counterpart. The time-crystalline character is consistently established through the mean-field analysis, exact calulation of Liouvillian spectra at finite size, and temporal correlations with cumulant expansion. Our work establishes a route to dissipative time-crystalline order in which coupling between simple two-level subsystems generates the effective internal structure otherwise provided by multilevel constituents.

</details>


### [118] [Deploying and validating a metropolitan QKD secure network: architecture and field performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11727)
*Claudia De Lazzari,Nicola Biagi,Damiano Giani,Marco Russo,Fernando Chirici,Francesco Stocco,Saverio Francesconi,Giacomo Ferranti,Alessandro Soureal,Antonella Sanguineti,Bartolomeo Montrucchio,Christian Laurenzi,Oliviero Testa,Guglielmo Morgari,Antonio Manzalini,Tommaso Occhipinti,Alessandro Zavatta,Davide Bacco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告了在米兰数据中心之间部署并实验验证了一个城域量子安全网络。该网络基于现有光纤基础设施运行，采用分层架构集成QKD硬件、符合标准的密钥管理（KM）和集中式软件定义网络（SDN）编排。通过主动光交换和可信节点路由实现动态路径重配置与自动故障切换。应用层验证确认了所有系统组件的无缝互操作性，证明了城域QKD网络在生产部署中的技术和操作就绪性，为构建城域量子安全通信基础设施提供了可复制的蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机对经典公钥基础设施构成生存威胁，而量子密钥分发（QKD）以信息论安全性解决密钥建立问题，不依赖任何计算复杂性假设。

Method: 在米兰数据中心间现有光纤基础设施上部署城域QKD网络，采用分层架构：QKD硬件层、符合标准的密钥管理层、集中式SDN编排层。通过主动光交换和可信节点路由实现动态路径重配置与自动故障切换。

Result: 跨多种协议和工作负载的应用层验证确认了所有系统组件的无缝互操作性。

Conclusion: 证明了城域QKD网络在生产部署中的技术和操作就绪性，提供了可复制的城域量子安全通信基础设施蓝图。

Abstract: The advent of cryptographically relevant quantum computers poses an existential threat to classical public-key infrastructure. Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) addresses this challenge by providing information-theoretic security for key establishment, independently of any computational hardness assumption. In this work, the deployment and experimental validation of a metropolitan-scale quantum-secure network between data centers in Milan is reported. The network operates over installed fiber infrastructure and implements a layered architecture integrating QKD hardware, standards-compliant Key Management (KM), and centralized Software-Defined Networking (SDN) orchestration. Dynamic path reconfiguration via active optical switching and trusted-node routing allow automated fail-over solutions. Application-layer validation across diverse protocols and workloads confirms the seamless interoperability of all system components. These results establish the technical and operational readiness of metropolitan QKD networks for production deployment, and offer a replicable blueprint for building quantum-secure communication infrastructure at metropolitan scale.

</details>


### [119] [Higher-order covariance matrices for non-Gaussian quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11759)
*Vojtěch Kala,Petr Marek,Nicolas J. Cerf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了高阶协方差矩阵，用于分析非高斯态在幺正变换下的行为，该方法在测量数据量、矩阵维度等方面具有显著优势，且维度随模数多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统辛框架仅基于一、二阶矩，不足以描述非高斯态的高阶矩特征。本文旨在提出一种能够捕捉非高斯态关键性质、且计算高效的高阶协方差矩阵方法。

Method: 从高阶正交单项式构建协方差矩阵，通过有限数量的正交角度的零差测量数据估计该矩阵，其维度不依赖于Fock基中的态跨度，且随模数多项式增长。

Result: 该方法能够简单有效地评估高斯变换对非高斯态的影响，可用于非线性压缩或非高斯零化子等问题，且矩阵维度适中，计算效率高。

Conclusion: 高阶协方差矩阵为分析非高斯态提供了一种实用且可扩展的数学框架，在实验实现上具有可行性。

Abstract: Covariance matrices lie at the heart of the powerful and well-established symplectic framework for describing continuous-variable Gaussian quantum states. However, since this framework only relies on first- and second-order moments, it is not sufficient for the analysis of non-Gaussian states because their higher-order moments are essential to capture some of their key properties. Here, we define higher-order covariance matrices -- more precisely, covariance matrices built from higher-order quadrature monomials -- which provide a simple way to evaluate the effect of Gaussian transformations on non-Gaussian states and can be used, for example, to address nonlinear squeezing or non-Gaussian nullifiers. Higher-order covariance matrices can be estimated from homodyne measurement data using only a limited number of quadrature angles, which involves matrices of moderate dimension compared with a full simulation in the Fock basis. The dimension of a higher-order covariance matrix does not depend on the span of the quantum states in Fock basis and, furthermore, scales only polynomially with the number of modes.

</details>


### [120] [Operational Concealment of Measurement Incompatibility by Quantum Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11762)
*Mohd Asad Siddiqui,Zizhu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了“操作隐藏”概念，描述测量不相容性在算子层面存在但在量子信道输出限制下不可访问的现象，并构建了基于伴随-核框架的系统理论，包括信道分类、鲁棒性度量、SDP公式、近似框架及二值量子比特POVM的几何刻画。证明了隐藏鲁棒性在非单射信道中可严格小于标准不相容鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量不相容性是量子力学的基本特征，但实践中观测受限于量子信道输出，导致不相容性可能被“隐藏”。现有理论缺乏对这种操作不可访问不相容性的系统刻画，需要建立统一框架来分类信道、量化隐藏程度并探索其应用。

Method: (1) 建立伴随-核框架，将可观测量按伴随信道核的等价类组织；(2) 基于核等价性和单调性对信道进行结构分类；(3) 定义隐藏鲁棒性度量并给出显式SDP公式；(4) 建立近似隐藏框架；(5) 对秩2单值量子比特信道下的无偏二值量子比特POVM给出几何刻画；(6) 通过解析族证明隐藏鲁棒性与标准不相容鲁棒性的关系。

Result: (1) 完成了信道基于核等价和单调性的结构分类；(2) 建立了隐藏鲁棒性的SDP可计算框架；(3) 证明了对单射信道，隐藏鲁棒性等于标准不相容鲁棒性；(4) 对非单射信道，隐藏鲁棒性可严格更小，并给出解析实例；(5) 提供了二值量子比特POVM在特定信道下的几何刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了操作不可访问的测量不相容性，为受限访问量子信息和半设备无关认证提供了定量工具，揭示了信道非单射性在隐藏不相容性中的关键作用。

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility can remain intact at the operator level yet become operationally inaccessible when observations are restricted to the output of a quantum channel; we refer to this phenomenon as operational concealment. We develop a systematic adjoint-kernel framework for operational concealment in which observables are organized into operational equivalence classes determined by the kernel of the adjoint channel. This framework yields a structural classification of channels via kernel equivalence and monotonicity, together with a concealment robustness measure admitting explicit SDP formulations. It also yields an approximate concealment framework and a geometric characterization of concealment for unbiased binary qubit POVMs under rank-2 unital qubit channels. We show that concealment robustness coincides with standard incompatibility robustness for injective channels but can be strictly smaller for non-injective channels, as demonstrated by explicit analytical families. These results provide a systematic characterization and quantitative treatment of operationally inaccessible measurement incompatibility, with implications for restricted-access quantum information and semi-device-independent certification.

</details>


### [121] [A new class of pure non-Gaussian quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11774)
*V. L. Gorshenin,B. N. Nougmanov,D. A. Chermoshentsev,I. A. Bilenko,F. Ya. Khalili*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有三角对称性的纯非高斯量子光场态，称为“三角态”，并展示了通过非简并四波混频过程结合后续信号模的光子数测量来生成这些态的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索和生成一类新的非高斯量子光场态，这类态在相平面上具有三角对称性，以扩展量子光学的态空间并可能用于量子信息处理。

Method: 利用标准的非简并四波混频过程，随后对两个信号模之一进行光子数符合测量（heralding measurement），从而制备出具有三角对称性的纯非高斯量子态。

Result: 定义了“三角态”这一新的非高斯量子态类别，并证明了通过四波混频加后选择测量可有效生成这类态。

Conclusion: 三角态作为一种新的非高斯量子光场态，其生成方案可行且具有潜在的量子信息应用价值。

Abstract: We discuss a new class of pure non-Gaussian quantum states of light characterized by trigonal symmetry on the phase plane. We propose the term ``trigonal states'' for them and show that they can be generated using the standard non-degenerate four-wave process supplemented by the subsequent heralding measurement of the photon number in one of the two signal modes.

</details>


### [122] [Private Capacity of Quantum Channels Induced by Non-stabilizer Environmental States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11793)
*Chunhe Xiong,Sunho Kim,Long Long,Junde Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用量子卷积理论研究离散变量量子系统的私有容量，发现魔幻资源在其中起关键作用：稳定子环境态下私有容量为零，魔幻环境态下可为正，且私有容量受环境态魔幻量上界约束，但具有对称结构的魔幻态也可能导致容量消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究魔幻资源（magic resource）在量子信道私有容量中的作用，理解非稳定子资源如何影响量子通信的安全性。

Method: 基于近期提出的离散变量量子卷积理论框架，分析卷积信道的私有容量。

Result: 对于一大类卷积信道：（1）当固定环境态为稳定子态时，私有容量为零；（2）某些魔幻环境态下私有容量可非零；（3）离散分束器酉的私有容量受环境态魔幻量上界约束；（4）若环境态具有特定对称结构，即使存在魔幻性，私有容量也会消失。

Conclusion: 魔幻资源在量子通信中扮演重要角色，但仅凭魔幻性不足以保证非零私有容量，环境态的对称结构也会影响通信安全性。

Abstract: We investigate the private capacity of quantum channels using the recently proposed quantum convolution theory for discrete-variable quantum systems. We focus on the role of the magic resource played in this framework. Firstly, for a large class of convolutional channels, we find that the private capacity is zero if the fixed environmental state is a stabilizer state. Moreover, we show that the private capacity can be nonzero for some magic environmental states. Furthermore, we show that the private capacity of a discrete beam splitter unitary is upper-bounded by the amount of magic of the environmental state. In addition, if the environmental state exhibits a certain symmetric structure, even if it is magic, the corresponding private capacity will also vanish for a class of convolution. These results emphasize the role of magic resources in quantum communication

</details>


### [123] [Multiparameter Quantum Estimation in a Raman-Coupled Two-Qubit System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11823)
*Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了热平衡状态下拉曼耦合两量子比特系统中的多参数量子估计，推导了量子费舍尔信息矩阵的解析表达式，分析了同时估计温度和拉曼耦合强度的量子克拉美-罗下界，发现同时估计在适当参数区域优于独立估计，并揭示了最优工作区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索热平衡拉曼耦合两量子比特系统中同时估计温度和拉曼耦合强度的量子极限精度，以及多参数量子估计相比独立估计的潜在优势。

Method: 推导量子费舍尔信息矩阵的解析表达式，计算量子克拉美-罗下界，对比同时估计与独立估计策略的性能，分析热涨落与相干相互作用之间的竞争关系。

Result: 量子测温存在明确的最优温度窗口；拉曼耦合强度估计在低温和弱耦合区域显著增强；在适当参数区域内，同时估计性能优于独立估计。

Conclusion: 研究为拉曼耦合两量子比特系统提供了精度极限的解析见解，并识别出量子传感和量子测温的理想工作区间，展示了多参数量子计量的优势。

Abstract: We investigate multiparameter quantum estimation in a Raman-coupled two-qubit system at thermal equilibrium. Analytical expressions for the quantum Fisher information matrix are derived to characterize the simultaneous estimation of the temperature and Raman coupling strength. The corresponding quantum Cramér--Rao bounds are obtained and compared with those of individual estimation strategies. Our results reveal optimal operating regimes determined by the interplay between thermal fluctuations and coherent interactions. In particular, quantum thermometry exhibits a well-defined optimal temperature window, whereas the estimation of the Raman coupling strength is significantly enhanced in the low-temperature and weak-coupling regime. We further show that simultaneous estimation can outperform independent estimation within appropriate parameter regions, highlighting the advantages of multiparameter quantum metrology. These results provide analytical insights into the ultimate precision limits of Raman-coupled two-qubit systems and identify promising operating regimes for quantum sensing and quantum thermometry.

</details>


### [124] [Multiparameter Quantum Metrology in Molecular Dimers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11831)
*Omar Bachain,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了由两个偶极-偶极相互作用的两能级系统组成的分子二聚体中的多参数量子估计，重点关注温度和失谐的同时估计。通过向量化方法推导量子费舍信息矩阵，分析了同时估计和单独估计策略的精度极限。结果表明，在近共振和低温条件下，同时估计优于单独估计，此时量子相干性增强；而在失谐条件和温度升高时，这种优势逐渐减小。温度是控制两种估计精度的关键参数，提供了量子计量学的统一视角。这些发现突显了分子量子系统作为多参数量子传感的现实平台的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索分子二聚体这一具体量子系统中多参数同时估计的可行性及优势，特别是在温度与失谐两个参数的联合估计中，量子相干性对估计精度的作用机制。

Method: 采用向量化方法推导量子费舍信息矩阵，对比同时估计与单独估计两种策略的精度极限，分析不同温度与失谐条件下的估计性能差异。

Result: 在近共振和低温区间，同时估计优于单独估计；失谐增大和温度升高会削弱这种优势。温度是调控估计精度的核心参数。

Conclusion: 分子量子系统可作为多参数量子传感的现实平台，温度在量子计量学中扮演统一控制参数的角色。

Abstract: We investigate multiparameter quantum estimation in a molecular dimer composed of two dipole--dipole interacting two-level systems, focusing on the simultaneous estimation of temperature $T$ and detuning $Λ$. By employing a vectorization approach to derive the quantum Fisher information matrix, we analyze the precision limits of both simultaneous and individual estimation strategies. We show that simultaneous estimation outperforms the individual one in the near-resonant and low-temperature regime, where quantum coherence is enhanced, while its advantage is progressively reduced under detuned conditions and increasing temperature. Our results demonstrate that temperature acts as a key control parameter governing both estimation precision providing a unified perspective on quantum metrology. These findings highlight the potential of molecular quantum systems as realistic platforms for multiparameter quantum sensing.

</details>


### [125] [Input-Aware Dynamic Backdoor Attack Against Quantum Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11843)
*Junrui Zhang,Zemin Chen,Lusi Li,Mohammad Ghasemigol,Daniel Takabi,Rui Ning*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-DIBA，首个针对量子神经网络的输入感知动态后门攻击，通过联合训练经典触发器生成器和受害者QNN，并引入集成密度对比损失（在测量前对量子态进行模态平均对比），解决了测量压缩导致监督弱化和密度矩阵波动导致对比学习不稳定的问题，在多个数据集和架构上实现了高攻击成功率和抗防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子后门攻击依赖固定触发器，易被检测；而将经典输入感知动态后门迁移到QNN面临两大障碍：测量压缩导致对触发器生成器的监督弱化，以及个体密度矩阵随输入波动导致逐样本对比学习不稳定。

Method: Q-DIBA采用三模式小批量策略联合训练经典触发器生成器和受害者QNN，提出集成密度对比损失（Ensemble Density Contrastive Loss），在测量前对后ansatz量子态进行操作，对比模态平均后的密度矩阵而非单个样本，以提供稳定的量子级监督。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上多个QNN架构的实验表明，Q-DIBA实现了高干净准确率、高攻击成功率和跨触发器准确率。该攻击还能抵抗视觉检查、谱特征检测和微调等防御手段。

Conclusion: 输入感知的量子后门攻击是QNN安全部署的重要威胁，Q-DIBA证明了其有效性、隐蔽性和输入特异性，为量子机器学习安全研究提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) are a promising framework for quantum machine learning on near-term quantum devices, but their security risks remain insufficiently understood. Studies have shown that QNNs are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, yet existing quantum backdoors mostly rely on a fixed trigger shared by all poisoned inputs. This fixed-trigger design is a major weakness because many defenses detect or weaken the repeated patterns such triggers leave in data representations. Although input-aware dynamic backdoors have been studied in classical neural networks, transferring them to QNNs is difficult because quantum learning introduces new obstacles. In particular, measurement compresses the post-ansatz quantum state into a limited classical output, weakening supervision for a trigger generator, while individual density matrices fluctuate with the input and make per-sample contrastive learning unstable. To address these challenges, we propose Q-DIBA, the first input-aware dynamic backdoor attack for QNNs. Q-DIBA jointly trains a classical trigger generator and a victim QNN through a three-mode mini-batch strategy that supports clean behavior, attack activation, and trigger specificity. To provide stable quantum-level supervision, Q-DIBA introduces an ensemble density contrastive loss that operates on post-ansatz quantum states before measurement and contrasts mode-averaged density matrices rather than individual samples. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST across multiple QNN architectures show that Q-DIBA achieves high clean accuracy, strong attack success, and high cross-trigger accuracy, demonstrating effectiveness, stealthiness, and input specificity. The attack also remains resilient against defenses including visual inspection, spectral-signature detection, and fine-tuning, suggesting that input-aware quantum backdoors are an important threat to secure QNN deployment.

</details>


### [126] [Optimal tomography of bosonic and fermionic Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11847)
*Senrui Chen,Marco Fanizza,Filippo Girardi,Ludovico Lami,Francesco Anna Mele,Michael Walter,Freek Witteveen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了玻色子和费米子高斯量子态的样本复杂度问题，证明无论纯态或混合态，学习所需的拷贝数随模式数二次增长，且与能量无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯量子态在量子光学、多体物理、量子化学等领域至关重要，但其样本复杂度此前未被完全确定，填补这一理论空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 利用高斯酉变换的表示理论，并提出了随机纯化通道（random purification channel）的推广形式，以此推导样本复杂度上界。

Result: 玻色子和费米子高斯态（纯态或混合态）的样本复杂度均为O(m²)，其中m为模式数，且不依赖于能量约束。

Conclusion: 完整确定了高斯量子态学习的样本复杂度，为量子信息处理中相关任务提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The sample complexity is the minimum number of copies required to learn an accurate classical description of a quantum state. Bosonic and fermionic Gaussian quantum states are families of quantum states that play a key role in quantum science and technology, from quantum optics and many-body physics to quantum chemistry, quantum computing, and quantum information theory. Despite their importance, their sample complexity had not been fully determined. We settle this open problem and show that both bosonic and fermionic Gaussian states can be learned using a number of copies that scales quadratically in the number of modes, regardless of whether the state is pure or mixed, and independently of any energy bound on the state. We derive these results by using the representation theory of Gaussian unitaries and by putting forth a generalization of the random purification channel to this setting and beyond.

</details>


### [127] [Bounding Kirkwood-Dirac negativity of Gaussian processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11854)
*Luca Bianchi,Carlo Marconi,Riccardo Cioli,Jan Sperling*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kirkwood-Dirac准概率在Gaussian过程中的极值行为，推导了任意量子态在任意模态和测量下的负性上界，并证明了单模双测量下正交算符本征态饱和该上界，而纯Gauss态达到非平凡最小值，表明Gauss态足以实现非经典性的极值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kirkwood-Dirac负性作为非经典性的关键度量，其一般情况下的极值尚未明确，需要填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在Gaussian过程框架下，利用数学推导方法分析任意量子态的Kirkwood-Dirac准概率，推导负性的上界，并通过单模双测量情形下的具体量子态（正交算符本征态和纯Gauss态）进行验证。

Result: 得到了任意模态和测量数量下的负性上界；单模双测量下，正交算符本征态饱和上界，纯Gauss态达到非平凡最小值。

Conclusion: Gauss态足以实现非经典性的极值，为量子非经典性的表征提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability provides an operational representation of a quantum state, whose negativity serves as a measure of nonclassicality. Despite its fundamental importance, the extremal values of the Kirkwood-Dirac negativity are still unknown in the general case. We investigate the Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability of an arbitrary quantum state under Gaussian processes. In this setting, we derive an upper bound on the negativity for any number of modes and measurements. For a single mode and two measurements, we show that the eigenstates of the quadrature operators saturate this upper bound, while a nontrivial minimum is reached by pure Gaussian states. As a consequence, our results indicate that Gaussian states are sufficient to achieve extreme values of nonclassicality.

</details>


### [128] [Trotter error compensation with polylogarithmic precision and nested-commutator scaling without ancillas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11856)
*Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou,Ziruo Wang,Pei Zeng,Jinzhao Sun,Qi Zhao,Tom Gur,Tongyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出HNCC算法，通过截断BCH展开和随机Pauli旋转通道补偿高阶Trotter误差，在保持乘积公式优势的同时将电路规模对精度的依赖从多项式降低为多对数级别，采样成本为标准的$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积公式是哈密顿量模拟最实用的方法之一，无需辅助量子比特且误差界由嵌套对易子控制，但其电路规模随逆精度呈多项式增长。本文旨在克服这一精度瓶颈，同时保留乘积公式无需辅助量子比特的主要优势。

Method: 利用截断Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff展开将高阶Trotter误差表示为嵌套对易子的乘积，然后在超算子层面通过随机采样的Pauli旋转通道补偿这些误差，避免Hadamard测试和辅助量子比特。

Result: 对于$K$阶乘积公式，HNCC达到$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$采样复杂度，最大门数为$\mathcal{O}(N^{\frac{2}{2K+1}} (k g_0 t \log(1/\varepsilon))^{1+\frac{1}{2K+1}} k(Γ+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$，时间依赖性与$2K+1$阶乘积公式相同。有限资源估计表明，在周期性Heisenberg链上，HNCC每电路CNOT和$T$门数在所有乘积公式方法中最低。

Conclusion: HNCC实现了电路规模对精度的多对数依赖，且无需辅助量子比特，在资源效率上优于现有乘积公式方法。

Abstract: Product formulas are among the most practical approaches to Hamiltonian simulation, requiring no ancillary qubits and exhibiting error bounds governed by nested commutators rather than only by Hamiltonian norms. Their circuit size, however, scales polynomially with the inverse precision. We develop a high-order nested-commutator compensation (HNCC) algorithm that preserves the main advantages of product formulas while achieving polylogarithmic precision dependence in the circuit size and the standard $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ sampling cost. HNCC uses a truncated Baker--Campbell--Hausdorff expansion to represent high-order Trotter errors by products of nested commutators and compensates these errors at the superoperator level through randomly sampled Pauli-rotation channels, avoiding Hadamard tests and ancillary qubits. For a fixed $K$-th order product formula applied to a $k$-local Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits with $Γ$ Pauli terms and local interaction strength $g_0$, HNCC estimates $\operatorname{tr}[Oe^{-\mathrm{i} tH}ρe^{\mathrm{i} tH}]$ to additive precision $\varepsilon\|O\|$ using $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ repetitions and a maximum gate count per circuit of $\mathcal{O}(N^{\frac{2}{2K+1}} (k g_0 t \log(1/\varepsilon))^{1+\frac{1}{2K+1}} k(Γ+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$. The resulting time dependence matches that of a product formula of order $2K+1$. Finite-size resource estimates for the periodic Heisenberg chain indicate that HNCC achieves the lowest CNOT and $T$-gate counts per circuit among the product-formula-based methods considered.

</details>


### [129] [Noise Resilience of Quantum Key Distribution Protocols Secured Against Independent Attacks With One-Way Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11857)
*Adam Bílek,Ryszard Kukulski,Paulina Lewandowska,Łukasz Pawela,Zbigniew Puchała*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了单量子比特量子密钥分发（QKD）协议在独立窃听攻击和单向经典通信密钥蒸馏场景下的抗噪声能力，提出了一种基于噪声的度量标准来量化QKD协议效率，并比较了BB84、B92、E91和六态协议等单量子比特QKD协议的噪声容忍度。结果表明六态协议具有最强的抗噪声能力和更高的后选择效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化单量子比特QKD协议在独立窃听攻击和单向经典通信密钥蒸馏场景下的噪声容忍度，并比较不同协议的抗噪声性能。

Method: 提出一种基于噪声的度量标准来量化QKD协议效率，在该框架下分析允许Alice和Bob渐近建立安全密钥的最大噪声水平，并对提出的噪声度量与传统QBER度量进行分析比较。

Result: 确定了QKD允许蒸馏渐近安全密钥的噪声水平阈值；六态协议比其他分析的协议具有更强的抗噪声能力和更高的后选择效率。

Conclusion: 六态协议在所考虑的安全模型中展现出最强的鲁棒性；对提出的基于噪声的度量和传统QBER度量进行了分析。

Abstract: We investigate the resilience to noise of single-qubit quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols in the scenario of security against independent eavesdropping attacks and key distillation based on one-way classical communication. To this end, we introduce a noise-based metric that quantifies the efficiency of QKD protocols. Within this framework, we analyze the maximal noise levels that allow Alice and Bob to asymptotically establish a secure secret key. Using this assumption, we compare the noise tolerance of general single-qubit QKD protocols, in particular the BB84, B92, E91, and six-state protocols. Our main result determines the noise level threshold for QKD allowing one to distill an asymptotically secure secret key. Additionally, we demonstrate that the six-state protocol achieves the greatest resistance to noise while simultaneously yielding a higher post-selection efficiency than the other analyzed single-qubit protocols, confirming its robustness within the considered security model. Finally, we perform an analysis of the proposed noise-based metric and the conventional quantum bit error rate (QBER) metric.

</details>


### [130] [Paraparticles intrinsically exhibit Hardy-space breakdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11867)
*Kejun Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，非酉交换统计（paraparticles）会内禀性地破坏记忆核的Hardy空间解析性，导致Kramers-Kronig关系在耦合强度gc≈0.1时失效；而费米子和玻色子由于交换酉性，在任何耦合下都免疫这种破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非酉交换统计（paraparticles）是否以及如何影响开放量子系统记忆核的解析结构，从而揭示其与标准粒子统计（费米子/玻色子）在色散关系层面的本质区别。

Method: 利用Hardy空间解析性条件分析记忆核的Laplace变换；通过Schur引理证明度规η的“影子”性质（与所有双线性可观测量对易）；构造超出对称代数的耦合算符以暴露度规畸变；通过极点分析确定临界耦合强度。

Result: paraparticles的记忆核在耦合强度gc≈0.1时出现上半平面极点，破坏KK关系；而费米子/玻色子在任何耦合下均无此现象。度规η与单位矩阵的Frobenius范数相对偏差为0.51。

Conclusion: 非酉交换统计内禀性地破坏记忆核的解析性，这一区分是内在的而非参数选择的结果，在开放量子系统层面将paraparticles与普通粒子统计根本区分开来。

Abstract: The memory kernel of an open quantum system obeys Kramers--Kronig (KK) relations if and only if its Laplace transform is analytic in the upper half-plane -- a property known as Hardy-space analyticity. Here we show that non-unitary exchange statistics, the defining property of paraparticles, intrinsically breaks Hardy-space analyticity. The metric $η$ that guarantees a real closed-system spectrum for these particles necessarily differs from the physical Born inner product ($\|η- I\|_F / \|I\|_F = 0.51$) -- a mathematical consequence of the R-matrix's non-unitarity, not a parameter choice. This metric is a "shadow metric": Schur's lemma forces it to commute with every bilinear observable, making the distortion physically invisible in the closed system. But when the paraparticle is coupled to a bath, any coupling operator that lies outside the symmetry algebra -- that is, any interaction that sees the internal flavour structure -- exposes the distortion. The memory kernel then develops upper-half-plane poles at coupling $g_c \approx 0.1$, breaking standard dispersion relations before the closed-system spectrum complexifies. Fermions and bosons, whose exchange is unitary ($η= I$ as an analytic fact of the canonical anticommutation algebra), are immune at any coupling, because there is no distortion to expose. The violation is intrinsic: it distinguishes non-unitary exchange statistics from ordinary particle statistics at the level of the memory kernel's analytic structure.

</details>


### [131] [An efficient algorithm for approximate shadow Hamiltonian simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11882)
*Abhijit Chakraborty,Bharath Sambasivam,Karunya Shirali,Hunter Nelson,Mafalda Ramôa,Sophia E. Economou,Edwin Barnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阴影哈密顿量模拟的高效算法，通过系统地识别与目标可观测量最相关的算子代数元素，大幅减少了进行时间演化所需的量子比特寄存器大小，并提出了预定义基、Krylov基及混合三种剪枝方案，在自旋晶格系统中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间无关哈密顿量下实时模拟可观测量动力学时，精确编码量子态通常面临算子代数指数增长的瓶颈，现有方法对于相互作用系统的计算成本过高。

Method: 提出阴影哈密顿量模拟算法，在可观测量通过哈密顿量对易生成的算子代数层面工作，通过系统性剪枝识别最相关元素；设计了基于预定义算子基、构造Krylov基以及混合（在预定义基的剪枝代数内构建Krylov基）三种剪枝方案。

Result: 在一维和二维晶格自旋系统中对单点和多点关联函数进行了基准测试，验证了算法在降低量子比特需求方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 阴影哈密顿量模拟通过有控近似实现高效量子态编码，显著降低了量子资源需求，为相互作用系统的实时动力学模拟提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose an efficient algorithm based on shadow Hamiltonian simulation to approximately simulate the real-time dynamics of observables under time-independent Hamiltonians. Shadow Hamiltonian simulation works at the level of the operator algebra generated by the observables through commutators with the Hamiltonian. Exactly encoding the quantum state in this picture is generally inefficient for interacting systems due to the exponential growth of the operator algebra. Our algorithm overcomes this bottleneck by systematically identifying the elements of the algebra most relevant to the target observables. This targeted approach is a controlled approximation that yields a highly efficient quantum state encoding that substantially reduces the size of the qubit register required to perform the time evolution using the shadow Hamiltonian. We propose two main pruning schemes, one based on a predefined operator basis and another on a constructed Krylov basis. We also present a hybrid scheme that builds a Krylov basis within a pruned algebra in the predefined basis. We benchmark our algorithm using lattice spin systems in one and two dimensions, for both one- and higher-point correlators as observables.

</details>
